function saveBlobLikeMenu17(name,blob){
 try{
  if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.AndroidBridge.saveFile==='function'&&typeof directSaveBlob==='function')return directSaveBlob(name,blob);
  if(window.miladgeoNative&&typeof window.miladgeoNative.saveFile==='function'&&typeof directSaveBlob==='function')return directSaveBlob(name,blob);
 }catch(e){console.error('Menu17-style export bridge failed',e);}
 return saveBlobFile(name,blob);
}
function directSaveBlob(name,blob){
 name=String(name||'MILADGEO_EXPORT.bin').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]+/g,'_');
 try{
  if(window.AndroidBridge&&typeof window.AndroidBridge.saveFile==='function'&&blob){
   showToast('⏳ آماده‌سازی فایل...');
   var readPromise=(typeof blob.arrayBuffer==='function')
     ? blob.arrayBuffer()
     : new Promise(function(resolve,reject){try{var fr=new FileReader();fr.onload=function(){resolve(fr.result);};fr.onerror=reject;fr.readAsArrayBuffer(blob);}catch(e){reject(e);}});
   readPromise.then(function(buf){
     var bytes=new Uint8Array(buf), binary='', chunk=0x8000;
     for(var i=0;i<bytes.length;i+=chunk){binary+=String.fromCharCode.apply(null,bytes.subarray(i,Math.min(i+chunk,bytes.length)));}
     var base64=btoa(binary);
     return window.AndroidBridge.saveFile(name, blob.type||'application/octet-stream', base64);
   }).then(function(ok){
     if(ok!==false)showToast('✓ File saved: '+name);
     else showToast('Export cancelled');
   }).catch(function(e){
     console.error('Android save failed',e);
     browserSaveBlob(name,blob,false);
   });
   return true;
  }
  if(window.miladgeoNative&&typeof window.miladgeoNative.saveFile==='function'&&blob){
   showToast('⏳ آماده‌سازی فایل...');
   var readPromise2=(typeof blob.arrayBuffer==='function')
     ? blob.arrayBuffer()
     : new Promise(function(resolve,reject){try{var fr2=new FileReader();fr2.onload=function(){resolve(fr2.result);};fr2.onerror=reject;fr2.readAsArrayBuffer(blob);}catch(e){reject(e);}});
   readPromise2.then(function(buf){
     var bytes=Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf));
     return window.miladgeoNative.saveFile({name:name,bytes:bytes});
   }).then(function(ok){
     if(ok)showToast('✓ File saved: '+name);
     else showToast('Export cancelled');
   }).catch(function(e){
     console.error('Native save failed',e);
     browserSaveBlob(name,blob,false);
   });
   return true;
  }
  var url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;a.download=name;a.rel='noopener';a.style.display='none';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();
  setTimeout(function(){try{a.remove();}catch(e){}try{URL.revokeObjectURL(url);}catch(e){}},15000);
  showToast('✓ '+name+' آماده شد');
  return true;
 }catch(e){console.error('Export failed',e);showToast('Export failed');return false;}
}
/* Public export bridge for isolated Menu 17 runtime.
   Menu 17 lives in a separate IIFE, so it cannot see the private export helpers above. */
window.miladgeoDirectSaveBlob=directSaveBlob;
window.miladgeoExportBlob=saveBlobFile;
function downloadText(filename,content,type){return saveBlobFile(filename,new Blob([content],{type:type||'text/plain;charset=utf-8'}));}
function exportSelected(){
 if(!job.name){showToast('Set Job first');return false;}
 if(!points.length){showToast('No points to export');return false;}
 var fmtv=$("exportFormat")?$("exportFormat").value:'csv';
 if(fmtv==='csv')return exportCSV();
 if(fmtv==='txt')return exportTXT();
 if(fmtv==='kml')return exportKML();
 if(fmtv==='kmz')return exportKMZ();
 if(fmtv==='dxf')return exportDXF();
 if(fmtv==='landxml')return exportLandXML();
 if(fmtv==='json')return exportJSON();
 if(fmtv==='code')return exportCodeTXT();
 return false;
}
function exportJobSelected(){
 if(!job.name){showToast('Set Job first');return false;}
 if(!points.length){showToast('No points to export');return false;}
 var fmtv=$("jobExportFormat")?$("jobExportFormat").value:'csv';
 if(fmtv==='csv')return exportCSV();
 if(fmtv==='txt')return exportTXT();
 if(fmtv==='kml')return exportKML();
 if(fmtv==='kmz')return exportKMZ();
 if(fmtv==='dxf')return exportDXF();
 if(fmtv==='landxml')return exportLandXML();
 if(fmtv==='json')return exportJSON();
 if(fmtv==='code')return exportCodeTXT();
 return false;
}
function exportCSV(){
 var csv='Point,DP,X,Y,Z,Distance,Azimuth\n',i,p;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];csv+='"'+csvSafe(p.no)+'","'+csvSafe(p.dp||'')+'",'+p.x+','+p.y+','+p.z+','+(p.distance==null?'':p.distance)+','+(p.azimuth==null?'':p.azimuth)+(i===points.length-1?'':'\n');}
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.csv',new Blob(['\ufeff'+csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'}));return false;
}
function exportTXT(){
 var txt='JOB\t'+job.name+'\nPOINT\tDP\tX\tY\tZ\tDISTANCE\tAZIMUTH\n',i,p;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];txt+=String(p.no||'')+'\t'+String(p.dp||'')+'\t'+p.x+'\t'+p.y+'\t'+p.z+'\t'+(p.distance==null?'':p.distance)+'\t'+(p.azimuth==null?'':p.azimuth)+'\n';}
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.txt',new Blob([txt],{type:'text/plain;charset=utf-8'}));return false;
}
function xmlSafe(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&apos;');}
function exportKML(){
 if(!points.length){showToast('No points to export');return false;}
 var usable=[],bad=0,i,p;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){
   p=points[i];
   var x=Number(p.x),y=Number(p.y),z=Number(p.z)||0;
   if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)){bad++;continue;}
   usable.push({no:p.no,dp:p.dp||'',x:x,y:y,z:z});
 }
 if(!usable.length){showToast('No valid coordinate points');return false;}
 var geographic=true;
 for(i=0;i<usable.length;i++){
   if(Math.abs(usable[i].x)>180||Math.abs(usable[i].y)>90){geographic=false;break;}
 }
 var geoPts=[];
 if(geographic){
   for(i=0;i<usable.length;i++)geoPts.push(usable[i]);
 }else{
   var zone=window.prompt('مختصات بزرگ هستند و احتمالاً UTM هستند.\\nشماره زون UTM را وارد کنید (مثلاً 39):','39');
   if(zone===null)return false;
   zone=parseInt(zone,10);
   if(!isFinite(zone)||zone<1||zone>60){showToast('UTM Zone must be 1–60');return false;}
   var northern=window.confirm('آیا مختصات در نیمکره شمالی است؟\\nOK = شمالی / Cancel = جنوبی');
   var converted=[],skipped=0;
   for(i=0;i<usable.length;i++){
     var xx=usable[i].x,yy=usable[i].y;
     var e=xx,n=yy;
     if(!(e>=100000&&e<=900000&&n>=0&&n<=10000000)){
       if(yy>=100000&&yy<=900000&&xx>=0&&xx<=10000000){e=yy;n=xx;}
       else{skipped++;continue;}
     }
     var ll=utmToLatLon(e,n,zone,northern);
     if(!isFinite(ll.lat)||!isFinite(ll.lon)){skipped++;continue;}
     converted.push({no:usable[i].no,dp:usable[i].dp,x:ll.lon,y:ll.lat,z:usable[i].z});
   }
   if(!converted.length){showToast('No valid UTM points — check X/Y columns');return false;}
   geoPts=converted;
   bad+=skipped;
 }
 var k='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<kml xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/kml/2.2"><Document><name>'+xmlSafe(job.name)+'</name>\n';
 for(i=0;i<geoPts.length;i++){p=geoPts[i];k+='<Placemark><name>'+xmlSafe(p.no)+'</name><description>'+xmlSafe(p.dp)+'</description><Point><coordinates>'+p.x.toFixed(8)+','+p.y.toFixed(8)+','+p.z+'</coordinates></Point></Placemark>\n';}
 k+='</Document></kml>';
 var kmlBlob=new Blob([k],{type:'application/vnd.google-earth.kml+xml;charset=utf-8'}); if(isTelegramMiniApp())saveBlobFile(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.kml',kmlBlob); else directSaveBlob(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.kml',kmlBlob);
 showToast('KML آماده شد ✓'+(bad?' — '+bad+' point skipped':''));return false;
}
function dxfNum(v){return Number(v).toFixed(6);}
function exportDXF(){
 var d='0\nSECTION\n2\nHEADER\n0\nENDSEC\n0\nSECTION\n2\nENTITIES\n',i,p;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];
  d+='0\nPOINT\n8\nPOINTS\n10\n'+dxfNum(p.x)+'\n20\n'+dxfNum(p.y)+'\n30\n'+dxfNum(p.z)+'\n';
  d+='0\nTEXT\n8\nPOINT_LABELS\n10\n'+dxfNum(p.x)+'\n20\n'+dxfNum(p.y)+'\n30\n'+dxfNum(p.z)+'\n40\n2.5\n1\n'+String(p.no||'').replace(/\r?\n/g,' ')+'\n';
 }
 d+='0\nENDSEC\n0\nEOF\n';
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.dxf',new Blob([d],{type:'application/dxf;charset=utf-8'}));return false;
}
function gsi16DataWord(wi,info,sign,value){
 var raw=String(value==null?'':value);
 raw=raw.slice(0,16).padStart(16,'0');
 return '*'+String(wi).padStart(2,'0')+String(info==null?'00':info).padStart(4,'0')+String(sign||'+').slice(0,1)+raw;
}
function gsi16Scaled(v){
 var n=Number(v);
 if(!isFinite(n))return null;
 var scaled=Math.round(n*1000);
 var sign=scaled<0?'-':'+';
 var abs=Math.abs(scaled).toString();
 if(abs.length>16)return null;
 return {sign:sign,data:abs.padStart(16,'0')};
}
function gsi16PointId(v){
 var raw=String(v==null?'':v).trim();
 if(!raw)raw='0';
 raw=raw.replace(/\s+/g,'_').slice(0,16);
 return raw.padStart(16,'0');
}
// Leica GSI exporter adapted from the tested GSI converter in indexv40.html.
// JOB points are mapped to E/N/Z and exported as Leica fixed-coordinate records.
function mg180GsiField(wi,val,width,info,sign){
 var raw=String(val==null?'':val).replace(/[\r\n]/g,' ').slice(0,width).padStart(width,'0');
 return String(wi).padStart(2,'0')+String(info||'....').padEnd(4,'.').slice(0,4)+(sign||'+')+raw;
}
function mg180GsiCoordWord(wi,v,width){
 var n=Number(v);if(!isFinite(n))throw new Error('مختصات نامعتبر است (WI '+wi+')');
 var scaled=Math.round(Math.abs(n)*1000),data=String(scaled);
 if(data.length>width)throw new Error('مختصات برای '+(width===8?'GSI-8':'GSI-16')+' بزرگ است (WI '+wi+')');
 return String(wi).padStart(2,'0')+'....'+(n<0?'-':'+')+data.padStart(width,'0');
}
function mg180GsiFixedOpWord(width,blockNo){return '410'+String(blockNo).padStart(3,'0')+'+'+'9'.padStart(width,'0');}
function exportJobLeicaGSI(format){
 try{
  if(!job||!job.name){showToast('ابتدا JOB را فعال کن');return false;}
  if(!Array.isArray(points)||!points.length){showToast('نقطه‌ای برای خروجی وجود ندارد');return false;}
  var width=format==='GSI-8'?8:16,lines=[],valid=0;
  for(var i=0;i<points.length;i++){
   var p=points[i]||{},x=Number(p.x),y=Number(p.y),z=Number(p.z);
   if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)||!isFinite(z))continue;
   var id=String(p.no==null?'':p.no).trim()||String(i+1);
   // Match the tested Leica converter's control-point mask: 410 operation 9,
   // WI 42 point identifier, followed by WI 43/44/45 fixed E/N/Z coordinates.
   var words=[mg180GsiFixedOpWord(width,valid+1),mg180GsiField(42,id,width,'....','+'),
    mg180GsiCoordWord(43,x,width),mg180GsiCoordWord(44,y,width),mg180GsiCoordWord(45,z,width)];
   lines.push((width===16?'*':'')+words.join(' '));valid++;
  }
  if(!lines.length){showToast('هیچ نقطه معتبر برای خروجی GSI وجود ندارد');return false;}
  var ext=format==='GSI-8'?'GSI8':'GSI16';
  var filename=sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_'+ext+'_CONTROL.gsi';
  saveBlobLikeMenu17(filename,new Blob([lines.join('\r\n')+'\r\n'],{type:'text/plain;charset=us-ascii'}));
  showToast('✓ خروجی Leica '+format+' آماده شد · '+valid+' نقطه');
 }catch(e){console.error('JOB_LEICA_GSI_EXPORT',e);showToast(e&&e.message?e.message:'ساخت فایل GSI ناموفق بود');}
 return false;
}

function exportSandingGSI(){
 if(!job||!job.name){showToast('ابتدا JOB را فعال کن');return false;}
 if(!Array.isArray(points)||!points.length){showToast('نقطه‌ای برای خروجی وجود ندارد');return false;}
 var lines=[],i,p,x,y,z,sx,sy,sz;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){
   p=points[i]||{}; x=Number(p.x); y=Number(p.y); z=Number(p.z||0);
   if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)||!isFinite(z))continue;
   sx=gsi16Scaled(x); sy=gsi16Scaled(y); sz=gsi16Scaled(z);
   if(!sx||!sy||!sz){showToast('مختصات نقطه '+String(p.no||i+1)+' برای GSI16 بزرگ است');return false;}
   lines.push(gsi16DataWord('11','0001','+',gsi16PointId(p.no))+' '+
              gsi16DataWord('81','..00',sx.sign,sx.data)+' '+
              gsi16DataWord('82','..00',sy.sign,sy.data)+' '+
              gsi16DataWord('83','..00',sz.sign,sz.data));
 }
 if(!lines.length){showToast('نقطه معتبر برای GSI پیدا نشد');return false;}
 var name=sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_SANDING_GSI.txt';
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(name,new Blob([lines.join('\r\n')+'\r\n'],{type:'text/plain;charset=ascii'}));
 showToast('✓ Sanding GSI آماده شد');
 return false;
}
function showPlannedTotalStationFormat(name){
 showToast(String(name)+' — فرمت به‌زودی عملیاتی می‌شود');
 return false;
}
function exportLeicaGSI16(){
 if(!job||!job.name){showToast('ابتدا JOB را فعال کن');return false;}
 if(!Array.isArray(points)||!points.length){showToast('نقطه‌ای برای خروجی وجود ندارد');return false;}
 var lines=[],i,p,x,y,z,sx,sy,sz;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){
   p=points[i]||{};
   x=Number(p.x); y=Number(p.y); z=Number(p.z||0);
   if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)||!isFinite(z))continue;
   sx=gsi16Scaled(x); sy=gsi16Scaled(y); sz=gsi16Scaled(z);
   if(!sx||!sy||!sz){showToast('مختصات نقطه '+String(p.no||i+1)+' برای GSI16 بزرگ است');return false;}
   lines.push(gsi16DataWord('11','0001','+',gsi16PointId(p.no))+' '+
              gsi16DataWord('81','..00',sx.sign,sx.data)+' '+
              gsi16DataWord('82','..00',sy.sign,sy.data)+' '+
              gsi16DataWord('83','..00',sz.sign,sz.data));
 }
 if(!lines.length){showToast('نقطه معتبر برای GSI16 پیدا نشد');return false;}
 var name=sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_GSI16.gsi';
 var blob=new Blob([lines.join('\r\n')+'\r\n'],{type:'text/plain;charset=ascii'});
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(name,blob);
 showToast('✓ Leica GSI16 آماده شد');
 return false;
}
function exportLandXML(){
 var x='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<LandXML version="1.2" date="'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'" time="'+new Date().toTimeString().slice(0,8)+'" xmlns="http://www.landxml.org/schema/LandXML-1.2" language="English" readOnly="false"><Project name="'+xmlSafe(job.name)+'"/><CgPoints>\n',i,p;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];x+='<CgPoint name="'+xmlSafe(p.no)+'" oID="'+xmlSafe(p.no)+'" desc="'+xmlSafe(p.dp||'')+'">'+p.y+' '+p.x+' '+p.z+'</CgPoint>\n';}
 x+='</CgPoints></LandXML>';
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.xml',new Blob([x],{type:'application/xml;charset=utf-8'}));return false;
}
function crc32(bytes){
 var table=crc32.table,crc=0xFFFFFFFF,i,j;
 if(!table){table=[];for(i=0;i<256;i++){var c=i;for(j=0;j<8;j++)c=(c&1)?(0xEDB88320^(c>>>1)):(c>>>1);table[i]=c>>>0;}crc32.table=table;}
 for(i=0;i<bytes.length;i++)crc=(table[(crc^bytes[i])&255]^(crc>>>8))>>>0;
 return (crc^0xFFFFFFFF)>>>0;
}
function putU16(a,o,v){a[o]=v&255;a[o+1]=(v>>>8)&255;}
function putU32(a,o,v){a[o]=v&255;a[o+1]=(v>>>8)&255;a[o+2]=(v>>>16)&255;a[o+3]=(v>>>24)&255;}
function makeKMZ(kml){
 var enc=new TextEncoder(),data=enc.encode(kml),name=enc.encode('doc.kml'),crc=crc32(data),n=data.length,total=30+name.length+n+46+name.length+22;
 var out=new Uint8Array(total),o=0;
 // Local file header
 putU32(out,o,0x04034b50);putU16(out,o+4,20);putU16(out,o+6,0);putU16(out,o+8,0);putU16(out,o+10,0);putU16(out,o+12,0);putU32(out,o+14,crc);putU32(out,o+18,n);putU32(out,o+22,n);putU16(out,o+26,name.length);putU16(out,o+28,0);o+=30;out.set(name,o);o+=name.length;out.set(data,o);o+=n;
 // Central directory
 var cd=o;putU32(out,o,0x02014b50);putU16(out,o+4,20);putU16(out,o+6,20);putU16(out,o+8,0);putU16(out,o+10,0);putU16(out,o+12,0);putU16(out,o+14,0);putU32(out,o+16,crc);putU32(out,o+20,n);putU32(out,o+24,n);putU16(out,o+28,name.length);putU16(out,o+30,0);putU16(out,o+32,0);putU16(out,o+34,0);putU16(out,o+36,0);putU32(out,o+38,0);putU32(out,o+42,0);o+=46;out.set(name,o);o+=name.length;
 // End of central directory
 putU32(out,o,0x06054b50);putU16(out,o+4,0);putU16(out,o+6,0);putU16(out,o+8,1);putU16(out,o+10,1);putU32(out,o+12,name.length+46);putU32(out,o+16,cd);putU16(out,o+20,0);
 return out;
}
function exportKMZ(){
 if(!points.length){showToast('No points to export');return false;}
 /* KMZ uses the same coordinate logic as KML: geographic stays as-is; UTM is converted. */
 var usable=[],i,p,bad=0;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];var x=Number(p.x),y=Number(p.y);if(isFinite(x)&&isFinite(y))usable.push({no:p.no,dp:p.dp||'',x:x,y:y,z:Number(p.z)||0});else bad++;}
 if(!usable.length){showToast('No valid coordinate points');return false;}
 var geographic=true;for(i=0;i<usable.length;i++){if(Math.abs(usable[i].x)>180||Math.abs(usable[i].y)>90){geographic=false;break;}}
 var geoPts=[];
 if(geographic) geoPts=usable;
 else{
   var zone=window.prompt('مختصات بزرگ هستند و احتمالاً UTM هستند.\\nشماره زون UTM را وارد کنید (مثلاً 39):','39');if(zone===null)return false;zone=parseInt(zone,10);if(!isFinite(zone)||zone<1||zone>60){showToast('UTM Zone must be 1–60');return false;}
   var northern=window.confirm('آیا مختصات در نیمکره شمالی است؟\\nOK = شمالی / Cancel = جنوبی');
   for(i=0;i<usable.length;i++){var xx=usable[i].x,yy=usable[i].y,e=xx,n=yy;if(!(e>=100000&&e<=900000&&n>=0&&n<=10000000)){if(yy>=100000&&yy<=900000&&xx>=0&&xx<=10000000){e=yy;n=xx;}else{bad++;continue;}}var ll=utmToLatLon(e,n,zone,northern);if(isFinite(ll.lat)&&isFinite(ll.lon))geoPts.push({no:usable[i].no,dp:usable[i].dp,x:ll.lon,y:ll.lat,z:usable[i].z});else bad++;}
 }
 if(!geoPts.length){showToast('No valid points for KMZ');return false;}
 var k='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<kml xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/kml/2.2"><Document><name>'+xmlSafe(job.name)+'</name>\n';
 for(i=0;i<geoPts.length;i++){p=geoPts[i];k+='<Placemark><name>'+xmlSafe(p.no)+'</name><description>'+xmlSafe(p.dp)+'</description><Point><coordinates>'+p.x.toFixed(8)+','+p.y.toFixed(8)+','+p.z+'</coordinates></Point></Placemark>\n';}
 k+='</Document></kml>';
 if(typeof TextEncoder==='undefined'){showToast('KMZ not supported here — use KML');return false;}
 var bytes=makeKMZ(k),blob=new Blob([bytes],{type:'application/vnd.google-earth.kmz'});if(isTelegramMiniApp())saveBlobFile(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.kmz',blob);else directSaveBlob(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.kmz',blob);showToast('KMZ آماده شد ✓'+(bad?' — '+bad+' point skipped':''));return false;
}
function exportCodeTXT(){
 var txt='POINT\tDP\tX\tY\tZ\n',i,p;
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];txt+=String(p.no||'')+'\t'+String(p.dp||'')+'\t'+p.x+'\t'+p.y+'\t'+p.z+'\n';}
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINT_CODE.txt',new Blob([txt],{type:'text/plain;charset=utf-8'}));return false;
}
function exportJSON(){
 var payload={job:job,station:setup,points:points};
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.json',new Blob([JSON.stringify(payload,null,2)],{type:'application/json;charset=utf-8'}));return false;
}

/* ===== V5 POINT TEXT IMPORT / FIELD EXPORT ===== */
var v5ImportText='';
var v5ImportedFileKeys={};
function v10HashText(text){var h=2166136261>>>0,i,c;for(i=0;i<text.length;i++){c=text.charCodeAt(i);h^=c;h=Math.imul(h,16777619)>>>0;}return ('00000000'+h.toString(16)).slice(-8);}
function v10ImportRegistryKey(){return v10HashText(String(v5ImportText||''));}
function v10LoadImportRegistry(){try{var raw=sessionStorage.getItem('miladgeo_v5_imported_files_'+JOB_KEY);if(raw){var a=JSON.parse(raw);if(a&&typeof a==='object')v5ImportedFileKeys=a;}}catch(e){v5ImportedFileKeys={};}}
function v10SaveImportRegistry(){try{sessionStorage.setItem('miladgeo_v5_imported_files_'+JOB_KEY,JSON.stringify(v5ImportedFileKeys));}catch(e){}}
v10LoadImportRegistry();
function v5LoadTextFile(input){
 try{var f=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(!f)return;var r=new FileReader();r.onload=function(e){v5ImportText=String(e.target.result||'').replace(/^\uFEFF/,'');var st=$("v5ImportStatus");if(st)st.innerHTML='<span class="v5-ok">✓ '+escapeHTML(f.name)+'</span> — '+v5ImportText.split(/\r?\n/).filter(function(x){return x.trim()!=='';}).length+' lines';v5PreviewImport();};r.onerror=function(){showToast('Could not read text file');};r.readAsText(f);}
 catch(e){showToast('Text file read error');}
}
function v5DetectDelimiter(line){
 if(line.indexOf('\t')>=0)return '\t';
 if(line.indexOf(',')>=0)return ',';
 if(line.indexOf(';')>=0)return ';';
 return 'space';
}
function v5Split(line,delim){
 line=String(line||'').trim();
 if(!line)return [];
 if(delim==='space')return line.split(/\s+/);
 return line.split(delim).map(function(x){return x.trim();});
}
function v5Num(v){var s=String(v==null?'':v).trim().replace(/^\(|\)$/g,'');if(s==='')return NaN;return Number(s.replace(/,/g,''));}
function v5LooksHeader(a){if(!a||a.length<3)return false;var z=a.slice(0,5).join(' ').toLowerCase();var headerWord=/\b(point|pointno|pointnumber|easting|northing|east|north|elev|elevation|height|code|desc|description|x|y|z)\b/.test(z);var numericCore=a.length>=4&&!isNaN(v5Num(a[1]))&&!isNaN(v5Num(a[2]))&&!isNaN(v5Num(a[3]));return headerWord&&!numericCore;}
function v5ParseText(text,forced){
 var lines=String(text||'').split(/\r?\n/).map(function(x){return x.trim();}).filter(function(x){return x!=='' && x.charAt(0)!=='#' && x.charAt(0)!==';'});
 if(!lines.length)return {rows:[],bad:0,delimiter:'-',format:'-'};
 var first=lines[0],delim=v5DetectDelimiter(first),format=forced&&forced!=='auto'?forced:'auto';
 var start=0,rows=[],bad=0;
 if(v5LooksHeader(v5Split(first,delim)))start=1;
 if(format==='auto'){
   var a=v5Split(lines[start]||'',delim), nums=a.map(v5Num);
   if(a.length>=5 && !isNaN(nums[1]) && !isNaN(nums[2]) && !isNaN(nums[3])){
     format='penzd';
     if(/north/i.test(a[1])||/northing/i.test(a[1]))format='pnezd';
   }else if(a.length>=4 && !isNaN(nums[1]) && !isNaN(nums[2]) && !isNaN(nums[3]))format='penzd';
   else if(a.length>=3 && !isNaN(nums[0]) && !isNaN(nums[1]) && !isNaN(nums[2]))format='xyz';
 }
 for(var i=start;i<lines.length;i++){
   var parts=v5Split(lines[i],delim);if(!parts.length)continue;
   var pno='',dp='',x=NaN,y=NaN,z=0;
   if(format==='penzd' || format==='pnezd'){
     if(parts.length<4){bad++;continue;}
     pno=parts[0];var a=v5Num(parts[1]),b=v5Num(parts[2]);z=v5Num(parts[3]);
     if(format==='penzd'){x=a;y=b;}else{y=a;x=b;}
     dp=parts.slice(4).join(delim==='space'?' ':' ');
   }else if(format==='nez' || format==='enz'){
     if(parts.length<3){bad++;continue;}var a2=v5Num(parts[0]),b2=v5Num(parts[1]);z=v5Num(parts[2]);
     if(format==='nez'){y=a2;x=b2;}else{x=a2;y=b2;}
     pno='P'+String(rows.length+1).padStart(3,'0');dp=parts.slice(3).join(' ');
   }else if(format==='xyz'){
     if(parts.length<3){bad++;continue;}x=v5Num(parts[0]);y=v5Num(parts[1]);z=v5Num(parts[2]);pno=parts[3]||('P'+String(rows.length+1).padStart(3,'0'));dp=parts.slice(4).join(' ');
   }else{bad++;continue;}
   if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)||!isFinite(z)){bad++;continue;}
   rows.push({no:pno||('P'+String(rows.length+1).padStart(3,'0')),dp:dp||'',x:x,y:y,z:z});
 }
 return {rows:rows,bad:bad,delimiter:delim,format:format,lines:lines.length};
}
function v5PreviewImport(){
 if(!v5ImportText){showToast('Choose a TXT/CSV file first');return false;}
 var f=$("v5ImportFormat")?$("v5ImportFormat").value:'auto',p=v5ParseText(v5ImportText,f),pre=$("v5ImportPreview"),st=$("v5ImportStatus");
 if(st)st.innerHTML='<span class="v5-ok">✓ DETECTED: '+escapeHTML(p.format.toUpperCase())+'</span> — delimiter: '+(p.delimiter==='space'?'SPACE':p.delimiter==='\t'?'TAB':escapeHTML(p.delimiter))+' — valid: '+p.rows.length+' — bad: '+p.bad;
 if(pre){pre.style.display='block';pre.textContent=p.rows.slice(0,20).map(function(r){return r.no+' | E/X '+r.x+' | N/Y '+r.y+' | Z '+r.z+(r.dp?' | '+r.dp:'');}).join('\n')+(p.rows.length>20?'\n...':'');}
 /* V13: Preview is also the import action. This keeps the workflow to two buttons:
    PREVIEW / IMPORT and CLEAR LIST, with no confusing second import button. */
 if(!p.rows.length){showToast('No valid survey points found');return false;}
 return v5ImportPoints();
}
function v9PointKey(no,x,y,z,dp){
 return [String(no||'').trim().toUpperCase(),Number(x).toFixed(6),Number(y).toFixed(6),Number(z).toFixed(6),String(dp||'').trim().toUpperCase()].join('|');
}
function v5ImportPoints(){
 if(!v5ImportText){showToast('Choose a TXT/CSV file first');return false;}
 var f=$("v5ImportFormat")?$("v5ImportFormat").value:'auto',p=v5ParseText(v5ImportText,f);
 if(!p.rows.length){showToast('No valid survey points found');return false;}
 var importKey=v10ImportRegistryKey();
 if(v5ImportedFileKeys[importKey]){showToast('این فایل قبلاً برای این JOB وارد شده است ✓');return false;}
 var autoNo=$("v5PointNoMode")&&$("v5PointNoMode").value==='auto',base=getNextPointNumber(),existingNo={},existingCoord={};
 for(var e=0;e<points.length;e++){var ep=points[e];existingNo[String(ep.no||'').trim().toUpperCase()]=true;existingCoord=[Number(ep.x).toFixed(6),Number(ep.y).toFixed(6),Number(ep.z).toFixed(6)].join('|');existingCoord=existingCoord;}
 /* Build coordinate keys separately; DP is intentionally ignored so the same survey point cannot be duplicated. */
 var coordSet={};
 for(var e2=0;e2<points.length;e2++){var ep2=points[e2];coordSet[[Number(ep2.x).toFixed(6),Number(ep2.y).toFixed(6),Number(ep2.z).toFixed(6)].join('|')]=true;}
 var added=0,dupes=0;
 for(var i=0;i<p.rows.length;i++){
   var r=p.rows[i],no=autoNo?'P'+String(base+added).padStart(3,'0'):String(r.no||('P'+String(base+added).padStart(3,'0')));
   var coordKey=[Number(r.x).toFixed(6),Number(r.y).toFixed(6),Number(r.z).toFixed(6)].join('|');
   var noKey=no.trim().toUpperCase();
   if(coordSet[coordKey] || existingNo[noKey]){dupes++;continue;}
   points.push({id:Date.now()+Math.floor(Math.random()*100000)+i,no:no,dp:r.dp||'',x:r.x,y:r.y,z:r.z,distance:null,azimuth:null,textImport:true});
   coordSet[coordKey]=true;existingNo[noKey]=true;added++;
 }
 v5ImportedFileKeys[importKey]=true;v10SaveImportRegistry();
 window.points=points;saveJSON(POINTS_KEY,points);refreshPoints();updateAllUI();try{window.m4pRender&&window.m4pRender();window.m4pRenderControl&&window.m4pRenderControl();window.m4pRenderStats&&window.m4pRenderStats();}catch(e){}
 showToast(added+' point(s) imported ✓'+(dupes?' — '+dupes+' duplicate point(s) skipped':'')+(p.bad?' — '+p.bad+' bad row(s) skipped':'')+(added===0?' — file marked as imported':'') );
 return false;
}

window.miladgeoImportTextIntoActiveJob=function(text){
 v5ImportText=String(text||'').replace(/^\uFEFF/,'');
 if(!v5ImportText.trim()){showToast('فایل خالی است');return false;}
 return v5ImportPoints();
};

function v5BuildDelimited(delim,mode){
 var out=[],i,p,head='';
 for(i=0;i<points.length;i++){p=points[i];var no=String(p.no||''),e=Number(p.x),n=Number(p.y),z=Number(p.z);if(mode==='pnezd')out.push([no,n.toFixed(4),e.toFixed(4),z.toFixed(4),String(p.dp||'')]);else out.push([no,e.toFixed(4),n.toFixed(4),z.toFixed(4),String(p.dp||'')]);}
 return out.map(function(a){return a.map(function(v){var s=String(v);if(delim===','&&/[",\n]/.test(s))s='"'+s.replace(/"/g,'""')+'"';return s;}).join(delim);}).join('\r\n')+'\r\n';
}
function exportPointsXLSX(){ if(!Array.isArray(points)||!points.length){showToast('ابتدا نقاط را در منو ۱۰ بارگذاری کن');return false;}
 try{
  /* Build a standards-compliant XLSX package locally. XLSX is a ZIP package;
     using stored ZIP entries avoids a CDN/library dependency on Android WebView. */
  var rows=[['Point','Easting','Northing','Elevation','Description','Distance','Azimuth']];
  points.forEach(function(p){rows.push([p.no==null?'':p.no,p.x==null?'':Number(p.x),p.y==null?'':Number(p.y),p.z==null?'':Number(p.z),p.dp||'',p.distance==null?'':Number(p.distance),p.azimuth==null?'':Number(p.azimuth)]);});
  function xmlEsc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&apos;');}
  function colName(n){var out='';while(n>0){var r=(n-1)%26;out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out;n=Math.floor((n-1)/26);}return out;}
  var sheetRows=rows.map(function(row,ri){var cells=row.map(function(v,ci){var ref=colName(ci+1)+(ri+1);if(typeof v==='number'&&isFinite(v))return '<c r="'+ref+'"><v>'+String(v)+'</v></c>';var str=String(v==null?'':v);return '<c r="'+ref+'" t="inlineStr"><is><t xml:space="preserve">'+xmlEsc(str)+'</t></is></c>';}).join('');return '<row r="'+(ri+1)+'">'+cells+'</row>';}).join('');
  var sheet='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><worksheet xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main"><cols><col min="1" max="1" width="16" customWidth="1"/><col min="2" max="4" width="18" customWidth="1"/><col min="5" max="5" width="30" customWidth="1"/><col min="6" max="7" width="16" customWidth="1"/></cols><sheetData>'+sheetRows+'</sheetData></worksheet>';
  var files=[
   ['[Content_Types].xml','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"><Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/><Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/workbook.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/></Types>'],
   ['_rels/.rels','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="xl/workbook.xml"/></Relationships>'],
   ['xl/workbook.xml','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><workbook xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main" xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"><sheets><sheet name="Points" sheetId="1" r:id="rId1"/></sheets></workbook>'],
   ['xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels','<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/worksheet" Target="worksheets/sheet1.xml"/></Relationships>'],
   ['xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml',sheet]
  ];
  function utf8(str){return new TextEncoder().encode(str);}
  function u16(n){return [n&255,(n>>>8)&255];}function u32(n){return [n&255,(n>>>8)&255,(n>>>16)&255,(n>>>24)&255];}
  var crcTable=exportPointsXLSX._crcTable;if(!crcTable){crcTable=[];for(var n=0;n<256;n++){var c=n;for(var k=0;k<8;k++)c=(c&1)?(0xEDB88320^(c>>>1)):(c>>>1);crcTable[n]=c>>>0;}exportPointsXLSX._crcTable=crcTable;}
  function crc32(bytes){var c=0xFFFFFFFF;for(var i=0;i<bytes.length;i++)c=crcTable[(c^bytes[i])&255]^(c>>>8);return (c^0xFFFFFFFF)>>>0;}
  var local=[],central=[],offset=0;
  files.forEach(function(f){var name=utf8(f[0]),data=utf8(f[1]),crc=crc32(data);var lh=[0x50,0x4b,3,4].concat(u16(20),u16(0),u16(0),u16(0),u16(0),u32(crc),u32(data.length),u32(data.length),u16(name.length),u16(0),Array.from(name));local.push(new Uint8Array(lh),data);var ch=[0x50,0x4b,1,2].concat(u16(20),u16(20),u16(0),u16(0),u16(0),u16(0),u32(crc),u32(data.length),u32(data.length),u16(name.length),u16(0),u16(0),u16(0),u16(0),u32(0),u32(offset),Array.from(name));central.push(new Uint8Array(ch));offset+=lh.length+data.length;});
  var centralSize=central.reduce(function(a,b){return a+b.length;},0),endRec=new Uint8Array([0x50,0x4b,5,6].concat(u16(0),u16(0),u16(files.length),u16(files.length),u32(centralSize),u32(offset),u16(0)));
  var blob=new Blob(local.concat(central,[endRec]),{type:'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet'});
  var fname=sanitizeFileName((typeof job!=='undefined'&&job&&job.name)||'MiladGeo')+'_POINTS.xlsx';
  saveBlobLikeMenu17(fname,blob);showToast('فایل اکسل واقعی XLSX آماده شد ✓');
 }catch(e){console.error('MENU10_REAL_XLSX_EXPORT',e);showToast('ساخت فایل اکسل ناموفق بود؛ گزارش خطا ثبت شد');}
 return false;
}
function exportPointsXLS(){if(!points.length){showToast('No points to export');return false;}if(typeof XLSX==='undefined'){showToast('Excel engine unavailable');return false;}try{var rows=points.map(function(p){return {Point:p.no||'',Easting:Number(p.x),Northing:Number(p.y),Elevation:Number(p.z),Description:p.dp||'',Distance:p.distance==null?'':Number(p.distance),Azimuth:p.azimuth==null?'':Number(p.azimuth)};}),ws=XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows),wb=XLSX.utils.book_new();XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,'Points');var out=XLSX.write(wb,{bookType:'xls',type:'array'});saveBlobLikeMenu17(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.xls',new Blob([out],{type:'application/vnd.ms-excel'}));showToast('XLS آماده شد ✓');}catch(e){console.error('XLS_EXPORT',e);showToast('ساخت XLS ناموفق بود؛ XLSX را استفاده کن');}return false;}
function exportGeoJSON(){if(!points.length){showToast('No points to export');return false;}var features=points.map(function(p){return {type:'Feature',properties:{Point:p.no||'',Description:p.dp||'',Z:Number(p.z)||0},geometry:{type:'Point',coordinates:[Number(p.x),Number(p.y),Number(p.z)||0]}};}),geo={type:'FeatureCollection',name:job.name||'MiladGeo Points',features:features};downloadText(sanitizeFileName(job.name)+'_POINTS.geojson',JSON.stringify(geo,null,2),'application/geo+json;charset=utf-8');showToast('GeoJSON آماده شد ✓');return false;}
function miladgeoPointListPDF(){
 /* INDEX90 — Menu 5 PDF is sent only through Menu 17's shared print engine.
    Keep one report source (the main project points array) for every point-list PDF button. */
 try{
  if(!Array.isArray(points)||!points.length){showToast('برای ساخت PDF ابتدا نقاط پروژه را ثبت یا وارد کن.');return false;}
  var safe=function(v){
   if(typeof escapeHTML==='function')return escapeHTML(v==null?'':String(v));
   return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];});
  };
  var rows=points.map(function(p){
   var x=Number(p.x),y=Number(p.y),z=Number(p.z);
   return '<tr><td>'+safe(p.no||'')+'</td><td>'+ (isFinite(x)?x.toFixed(4):'') +'</td><td>'+ (isFinite(y)?y.toFixed(4):'') +'</td><td>'+ (isFinite(z)?z.toFixed(4):'') +'</td><td>'+safe(p.dp||'')+'</td></tr>';
  }).join('');
  var jobName=(typeof job!=='undefined'&&job&&job.name)?job.name:'—';
  var html='<!doctype html><html lang="fa" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>MiladGeo — گزارش نقاط</title><style>'+
   '@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:10mm}*{box-sizing:border-box}html,body{margin:0;padding:0;background:#fff;color:#111}'+
   'body{font-family:Tahoma,Arial,sans-serif;font-size:10pt;direction:rtl;padding:4mm}h2{text-align:center;margin:0 0 7mm;font-size:16pt}'+
   '.meta{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 4mm;font-size:9pt}'+
   'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;direction:ltr;table-layout:auto;font-size:9pt}th,td{border:1px solid #555;padding:6px 5px;text-align:center;vertical-align:middle}'+
   'th{background:#e8ece4;font-weight:800}tbody tr:nth-child(even){background:#f6f8f5}thead{display:table-header-group}tr{break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid}'+
   '@media print{body{padding:0}h2{margin-top:0}}</style></head><body>'+
   '<h2>گزارش نقاط پروژه — MiladGeo</h2><div class="meta"><span>پروژه: '+safe(jobName)+'</span><span>تعداد نقاط: '+points.length+'</span><span>تاریخ: '+safe(new Date().toLocaleString())+'</span></div>'+
   '<table><thead><tr><th>Point</th><th>Easting / X</th><th>Northing / Y</th><th>Elevation / Z</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows+'</tbody></table></body></html>';

  /* This is the exact public bridge exposed by Menu 17's m17PrintHtml(). */
  var menu17Printer=window.miladgeoM17PrintHtml;
  if(typeof menu17Printer!=='function'){
   showToast('موتور PDF منو ۱۷ بارگذاری نشده است؛ خروجی ساخته نشد.');
   console.error('MENU5_PDF: Menu 17 print bridge is unavailable');
   return false;
  }
  var accepted=menu17Printer.call(window,html,'MiladGeo_Menu5_Point_Report.pdf');
  if(accepted===true){
   showToast('گزارش نقاط با موتور PDF منو ۱۷ ارسال شد؛ ذخیره به‌صورت PDF را انتخاب کن.');
   return false;
  }
  showToast('موتور PDF منو ۱۷ نتوانست گزارش را باز کند؛ اجازه چاپ یا بازشدن پنجره را بررسی کن.');
  console.error('MENU5_PDF: Menu 17 print engine returned false');
  return false;
 }catch(e){
  console.error('MENU5_PDF_ENGINE',e);
  showToast('ساخت PDF نقاط ناموفق بود: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:'خطای نامشخص'));
  return false;
 }
}
/* Public aliases: every point-list PDF entry point calls the SAME engine. */
function printPointsPDF(){return miladgeoPointListPDF();}

function v5ExportFormat(mode){
 if(!points.length){showToast('No points to export');return false;}
 var name=sanitizeFileName(job.name),txt='',file='',mime='text/plain;charset=utf-8';
 if(mode==='penzd'){txt=v5BuildDelimited(',','penzd');file=name+'_PENZD.txt';}
 else if(mode==='pnezd'){txt=v5BuildDelimited(',','pnezd');file=name+'_PNEZD.txt';}
 else if(mode==='nez'){txt=points.map(function(p){return Number(p.y).toFixed(4)+' '+Number(p.x).toFixed(4)+' '+Number(p.z).toFixed(4);}).join('\r\n')+'\r\n';file=name+'_NEZ.txt';}
 else if(mode==='enz'){txt=points.map(function(p){return Number(p.x).toFixed(4)+' '+Number(p.y).toFixed(4)+' '+Number(p.z).toFixed(4);}).join('\r\n')+'\r\n';file=name+'_ENZ.txt';}
 else if(mode==='csv'){txt='Point,Easting,Northing,Elevation,Description\r\n'+v5BuildDelimited(',','penzd');file=name+'_PENZD.csv';mime='text/csv;charset=utf-8';}
 else if(mode==='leica_ascii'){txt='Point,Easting,Northing,Height,Code\r\n'+v5BuildDelimited(',','penzd');file=name+'_LEICA_ASCII.csv';mime='text/csv;charset=utf-8';}
 else return false;
 saveBlobLikeMenu17(file,new Blob(['\ufeff'+txt],{type:mime||'text/plain;charset=utf-8'}));showToast(mode.toUpperCase()+' export ready ✓');return false;
}

function sanitizeFileName(v){return String(v).replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_')||'MILADGEO_JOB';}

function fmt(v,n){if(n==null)n=3;var x=Number(v);return isFinite(x)?x.toFixed(n):'-';}
function escapeHTML(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#039;');}
function showToast(m){var t=$("toast");if(!t)return;t.textContent=m;t.classList.add('show');clearTimeout(window.miladToastTimer);window.miladToastTimer=setTimeout(function(){t.classList.remove('show');},1900);}

tFile=v5LoadTextFile;window.v5PreviewImport=v5PreviewImport;window.v5ImportPoints=v5ImportPoints;window.v5ExportFormat=v5ExportFormat;window.addPENZDPoint=addPENZDPoint;window.clearPENZDInput=clearPENZDInput;window.exportPointsXLSX=exportPointsXLSX;window.exportPointsXLS=exportPointsXLS;window.exportGeoJSON=exportGeoJSON;window.printPointsPDF=printPointsPDF;window.updateJobOutput363=updateJobOutput363;
var v8Points=[],v8SurfaceCount=3,v8Last={mode:'',cut:0,fill:0,net:0,total:0,area:0,points:[]};
function v8Num(v){var x=Number(String(v==null?'':v).replace(/,/g,''));return isFinite(x)?x:NaN;}
function v8Mode(mode){var ids=['points','surfaces','grid','base','earth'];ids.forEach(function(x){var p=$('v8'+x.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+x.slice(1)+'Panel');if(p)p.style.display=x===mode?'block':'none';});document.querySelectorAll('.v8-tab').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active')});var ix=ids.indexOf(mode);var tabs=document.querySelectorAll('.v8-tab');if(tabs[ix])tabs[ix].classList.add('active');if(mode==='earth')v8RenderEarth();return false;}
function v8PointRow(p,i){p=p||{};return '<div class="v8-point-row"><div class="v8-pno">P'+i+'</div><input id="v8x'+i+'" type="number" step="any" value="'+(p.x==null?'':p.x)+'" placeholder="X"><input id="v8y'+i+'" type="number" step="any" value="'+(p.y==null?'':p.y)+'" placeholder="Y"><input id="v8z'+i+'" type="number" step="any" value="'+(p.z==null?'':p.z)+'" placeholder="Z"><button class="v8-btn v8-remove" type="button" onclick="v8RemovePoint('+i+');return false;">×</button></div>';}
function v8RenderPoints(){var b=$('v8Points');if(!b)return;b.innerHTML=v8Points.map(function(p,i){return v8PointRow(p,i+1)}).join('');}
function v8ReadPoints(){var out=[],i,x,y,z;for(i=1;i<=v8Points.length;i++){x=v8Num($('v8x'+i).value);y=v8Num($('v8y'+i).value);z=v8Num($('v8z'+i).value);if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)||!isFinite(z))continue;out.push({no:i,x:x,y:y,z:z});}return out;}
function v8AddPoint(){v8Points.push({x:'',y:'',z:''});v8RenderPoints();var e=$('v8x'+v8Points.length);if(e)e.focus();return false;}
function v8RemovePoint(i){if(v8Points.length<=3){showToast('حداقل ۳ نقطه لازم است');return false;}v8Points.splice(i-1,1);v8RenderPoints();return false;}
function v8ClearPoints(){v8Points=[{},{},{}];v8RenderPoints();$('v8Result').innerHTML='<div class="v8-empty">داده‌ها پاک شدند.</div>';v8Draw([]);return false;}
function v8LoadJob(){try{var src=(Array.isArray(points)?points:[]);if(!src.length){try{var raw=localStorage.getItem(POINTS_KEY);if(raw){var parsed=JSON.parse(raw);if(Array.isArray(parsed))src=parsed;}}catch(_e){}}if(!src.length){showToast('در جاب نقطه‌ای وجود ندارد');return false;}var a=src.map(function(p,i){return {x:v8Num(p.x),y:v8Num(p.y),z:v8Num(p.z),no:p.no||p.name||('P'+(i+1))}}).filter(function(p){return isFinite(p.x)&&isFinite(p.y)&&isFinite(p.z)});if(a.length<3){showToast('حداقل ۳ نقطه معتبر لازم است');return false;}v8Points=a;v8RenderPoints();showToast(a.length+' نقطه از جاب وارد شد ✓');return false;}catch(e){console.error('V8_LOAD_JOB',e);showToast('ورود از جاب انجام نشد');return false;}}
function v8ImportFile(input){var f=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(!f)return false;var r=new FileReader();r.onload=function(ev){try{var rows=[];if(/\.(xlsx|xls)$/i.test(f.name)&&window.XLSX){var wb=XLSX.read(new Uint8Array(ev.target.result),{type:'array'}),ws=wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];rows=XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{header:1,defval:''});}else rows=String(ev.target.result||'').split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean).map(function(l){return l.split(/[;,\t, ]+/)});var out=[];rows.forEach(function(row,i){var vals=row.map(v=>v);var nums=vals.map(v=>v8Num(v)).filter(function(x){return isFinite(x)});if(nums.length>=3){var x=v8Num(row[1]),y=v8Num(row[2]),z=v8Num(row[3]);if(!isFinite(x)||!isFinite(y)||!isFinite(z)){x=v8Num(row[0]);y=v8Num(row[1]);z=v8Num(row[2]);}if(isFinite(x)&&isFinite(y)&&isFinite(z))out.push({x:x,y:y,z:z,no:i+1});}});if(out.length<3){showToast('حداقل ۳ نقطه X/Y/Z معتبر لازم است');return;}v8Points=out;v8RenderPoints();showToast(out.length+' نقطه وارد شد ✓');}catch(e){console.error('V8_IMPORT',e);showToast('خواندن فایل انجام نشد');}};if(/\.(xlsx|xls)$/i.test(f.name))r.readAsArrayBuffer(f);else r.readAsText(f);return false;}
function v8PolyArea(a){var s=0,i,j;for(i=0;i<a.length;i++){j=(i+1)%a.length;s+=a[i].x*a[j].y-a[j].x*a[i].y;}return Math.abs(s)/2;}
function v8Tri(a,b,c){return Math.abs((b.x-a.x)*(c.y-a.y)-(c.x-a.x)*(b.y-a.y))/2;}
function v8CalcBase(){var a=v8ReadPoints();var rl=v8Num($('v8BaseRL').value);if(a.length<3||!isFinite(rl)){showToast('حداقل ۳ نقطه و RL معتبر لازم است');return false;}var area=v8PolyArea(a),cut=0,fill=0,vol=0;for(var i=1;i<a.length-1;i++){var ar=v8Tri(a[0],a[i],a[i+1]);var d=((a[0].z-rl)+(a[i].z-rl)+(a[i+1].z-rl))/3;var v=ar*Math.abs(d);vol+=v;if(d>0)cut+=v;else if(d<0)fill+=v;}v8Last={mode:'سطح مبنا',cut:cut,fill:fill,net:cut-fill,total:vol,area:area,points:a,rl:rl};v8ShowResult('حجم نسبت به سطح مبنا',area,vol,cut,fill,cut-fill,a.length);v8Draw(a);v8RenderEarth();return false;}
function v8CopyBaseAndCalc(){var v=$('v8BaseRL2').value;if($('v8BaseRL'))$('v8BaseRL').value=v;v8Mode('points');return v8CalcBase();}
function v8RenderSurfaceRows(){var a=[],i;for(i=1;i<=v8SurfaceCount;i++){a.push('<div class="v8-surface-row"><b>P'+i+'</b><input id="v8ex'+i+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="X"><input id="v8ey'+i+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="Y"><input id="v8ez'+i+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="Z"></div>');}var d=a.join('');if($('v8Existing'))$('v8Existing').innerHTML=d;if($('v8Design'))$('v8Design').innerHTML=a.map(function(_,i){return '<div class="v8-surface-row"><b>P'+(i+1)+'</b><input id="v8dx'+(i+1)+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="X"><input id="v8dy'+(i+1)+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="Y"><input id="v8dz'+(i+1)+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="Z"></div>'}).join('');}
function v8AddSurfaceRow(){v8SurfaceCount++;v8RenderSurfaceRows();return false;}
function v8ClearSurfaces(){v8SurfaceCount=3;v8RenderSurfaceRows();return false;}
function v8CalcSurfaces(){var a=[],b=[],i;for(i=1;i<=v8SurfaceCount;i++){var p={x:v8Num($('v8ex'+i).value),y:v8Num($('v8ey'+i).value),z:v8Num($('v8ez'+i).value)},q={x:v8Num($('v8dx'+i).value),y:v8Num($('v8dy'+i).value),z:v8Num($('v8dz'+i).value)};if(!isFinite(p.x)||!isFinite(p.y)||!isFinite(p.z)||!isFinite(q.x)||!isFinite(q.y)||!isFinite(q.z)){showToast('همه مختصات دو سطح را وارد کن');return false;}a.push(p);b.push(q);}var cut=0,fill=0,total=0,area=v8PolyArea(a);for(i=1;i<a.length-1;i++){var ar=(v8Tri(a[0],a[i],a[i+1])+v8Tri(b[0],b[i],b[i+1]))/2;var d=((a[0].z-b[0].z)+(a[i].z-b[i].z)+(a[i+1].z-b[i+1].z))/3;var v=ar*Math.abs(d);total+=v;if(d>0)cut+=v;else if(d<0)fill+=v;}v8Last={mode:'دو سطح',cut:cut,fill:fill,net:cut-fill,total:total,area:area,points:a,design:b};v8ShowResult('حجم بین دو سطح',area,total,cut,fill,cut-fill,a.length);v8Draw(a);v8RenderEarth();return false;}
function v8BuildGrid(){var c=Math.max(1,Math.min(20,parseInt($('v8Cols').value)||1)),r=Math.max(1,Math.min(20,parseInt($('v8Rows').value)||1)),h='';h+='<table class="v8-grid-table"><tr><th>ردیف / ستون</th>';for(var j=0;j<=c;j++)h+='<th>'+j+'</th>';h+='</tr>';for(var i=0;i<=r;i++){h+='<tr><th>'+i+'</th>';for(j=0;j<=c;j++)h+='<td><input id="v8g'+i+'_'+j+'" type="number" step="any" placeholder="Z"></td>';h+='</tr>';}h+='</table>';$('v8GridInputs').innerHTML=h;return false;}
function v8CalcGrid(){var c=Math.max(1,parseInt($('v8Cols').value)||1),r=Math.max(1,parseInt($('v8Rows').value)||1),w=v8Num($('v8CellW').value),h=v8Num($('v8CellH').value),rl=v8Num($('v8GridRL').value);if(!isFinite(w)||!isFinite(h)||!isFinite(rl)){showToast('ابعاد سلول و RL معتبر لازم است');return false;}var cut=0,fill=0,total=0;for(var i=0;i<r;i++)for(var j=0;j<c;j++){var z=[v8Num($('v8g'+i+'_'+j).value),v8Num($('v8g'+(i+1)+'_'+j).value),v8Num($('v8g'+i+'_'+(j+1)).value),v8Num($('v8g'+(i+1)+'_'+(j+1)).value)];if(z.some(function(x){return !isFinite(x)})){showToast('ارتفاع همه گره‌های شبکه را وارد کن');return false;}var d=(z[0]+z[1]+z[2]+z[3])/4-rl,v=w*h*Math.abs(d);total+=v;if(d>0)cut+=v;else if(d<0)fill+=v;}v8Last={mode:'شبکه',cut:cut,fill:fill,net:cut-fill,total:total,area:c*r*w*h,points:[]};v8ShowResult('حجم شبکه',c*r*w*h,total,cut,fill,cut-fill,c*r);v8RenderEarth();return false;}
function v8ShowResult(title,area,total,cut,fill,net,count){$('v8Result').innerHTML='<div class="v8-panel-title">✓ '+title+'</div><div class="v8-main-number">'+fmt(total,3)+' <small>m³</small></div><div class="v8-stat-grid"><div class="v8-stat"><small>مساحت</small><b>'+fmt(area,3)+' m²</b></div><div class="v8-stat"><small>خاکبرداری</small><b>'+fmt(cut,3)+' m³</b></div><div class="v8-stat"><small>خاکریزی</small><b>'+fmt(fill,3)+' m³</b></div><div class="v8-stat"><small>خالص</small><b>'+fmt(net,3)+' m³</b></div></div><div style="text-align:center;font-size:9px;margin-top:7px">تعداد داده: '+count+' — روش: '+title+'</div>';showToast('محاسبه حجم انجام شد ✓');}
function v8RenderEarth(){var e=$('v8EarthSummary');if(!e)return;e.innerHTML='<div class="v8-earth-box"><small>خاکبرداری CUT</small><b>'+fmt(v8Last.cut||0,3)+' m³</b></div><div class="v8-earth-box"><small>خاکریزی FILL</small><b>'+fmt(v8Last.fill||0,3)+' m³</b></div><div class="v8-earth-box"><small>حجم خالص</small><b>'+fmt(v8Last.net||0,3)+' m³</b></div>';}
function v8Draw(a){var svg=$('v8Sketch');if(!svg)return;if(!a||a.length<1){svg.innerHTML='';return;}var minx=Math.min.apply(null,a.map(function(p){return p.x})),maxx=Math.max.apply(null,a.map(function(p){return p.x})),miny=Math.min.apply(null,a.map(function(p){return p.y})),maxy=Math.max.apply(null,a.map(function(p){return p.y})),w=maxx-minx||1,h=maxy-miny||1,pad=45;function X(x){return pad+(x-minx)/(w)*(700-2*pad)}function Y(y){return 360-pad-(y-miny)/(h)*(360-2*pad)}var pts=a.map(function(p){return X(p.x)+','+Y(p.y)}).join(' ');svg.innerHTML='<text x="20" y="28">N</text><line x1="25" y1="60" x2="25" y2="28" stroke="#596b5a" stroke-width="2"/><polygon class="poly" points="'+pts+'"></polygon>'+a.map(function(p,i){return '<circle class="pt" cx="'+X(p.x)+'" cy="'+Y(p.y)+'" r="5"></circle><text x="'+(X(p.x)+7)+'" y="'+(Y(p.y)-7)+'">P'+(i+1)+'</text>'}).join('');}
function v8ReportHtml(){var j=(typeof job!=='undefined'&&job&&job.name)?job.name:'—';return '<!doctype html><html dir="rtl"><meta charset="utf-8"><style>body{font-family:Arial,Tahoma,sans-serif;padding:24px;color:#172018}h1{font-size:24px}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:14px}td,th{border:1px solid #bbb;padding:8px;text-align:center}th{background:#e1eadf}.box{padding:14px;border:1px solid #8ea08c;border-radius:10px;margin:10px 0}</style><h1>گزارش حرفه‌ای حجم — MiladGeo</h1><div class="box">JOB: '+escapeHTML(j)+'<br>روش: '+escapeHTML(v8Last.mode||'—')+'<br>تعداد داده: '+(v8Last.points||[]).length+'</div><table><tr><th>مورد</th><th>مقدار</th></tr><tr><td>حجم کل</td><td>'+fmt(v8Last.total||0,3)+' m³</td></tr><tr><td>خاکبرداری</td><td>'+fmt(v8Last.cut||0,3)+' m³</td></tr><tr><td>خاکریزی</td><td>'+fmt(v8Last.fill||0,3)+' m³</td></tr><tr><td>حجم خالص</td><td>'+fmt(v8Last.net||0,3)+' m³</td></tr></table></html>';}

function v8ExportKML(){var a=v8Last.points||[];if(a.length<3){showToast('برای KML حداقل ۳ نقطه لازم است');return false;}var zone=v8Num($('v8KmlZone')&&$('v8KmlZone').value),hem=$('v8KmlHem')?$('v8KmlHem').value:'N';if(!isFinite(zone)||zone<1||zone>60){showToast('زون UTM معتبر برای KML وارد کن');return false;}if(typeof window.m17ConvertUtmToWgs84!=='function'){showToast('مبدل مختصات KML آماده نیست');return false;}var coords=[];try{a.forEach(function(p){var c=window.m17UtmToWgs84?window.m17UtmToWgs84(p.x,p.y,zone,hem):window.m17ConvertUtmToWgs84(p.x,p.y,zone);coords.push(Number(c.lng).toFixed(8)+','+Number(c.lat).toFixed(8)+','+Number(p.z||0).toFixed(3));});coords.push(coords[0]);}catch(e){console.error('V8_KML',e);showToast('تبدیل مختصات KML ناموفق بود');return false;}var k='<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><kml xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/kml/2.2"><Document><name>MiladGeo Volume</name><Placemark><Polygon><outerBoundaryIs><LinearRing><coordinates>'+coords.join(' ')+'</coordinates></LinearRing></outerBoundaryIs></Polygon></Placemark></Document></kml>';return saveBlobLikeMenu17('MiladGeo_Volume.kml',new Blob([k],{type:'application/vnd.google-earth.kml+xml;charset=utf-8'}));}

function excelNorm(v){return String(v==null?'':v).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_\-\/]+/g,'');}
function excelPickHeader(headers,keys,exclude){var i,j,h,n;for(i=0;i<headers.length;i++){h=String(headers[i]);n=excelNorm(h);if(exclude&&exclude.indexOf(i)>=0)continue;for(j=0;j<keys.length;j++){if(n===excelNorm(keys[j])||n.indexOf(excelNorm(keys[j]))>=0)return i;}}return -1;}
function fillExcelSelect(id,selected,allowNone){var el=$(id);if(!el)return;var out=allowNone?'<option value="-1">— NONE —</option>':'';for(var i=0;i<excelHeaders.length;i++)out+='<option value="'+i+'">'+escapeHTML(String(excelHeaders[i]))+'</option>';el.innerHTML=out;if(selected>=0&&selected<excelHeaders.length)el.value=String(selected);}
function setupExcelMapping(){
  /* V48: no manual column selectors. Standard survey formats are handled
     automatically. With headers, names are detected first; without headers,
     the selected survey format is interpreted positionally. */
  if(!excelHeaders.length)return;
  var fmt=$('excelFormat')?$('excelFormat').value:'PENZD';
  var hm=$('excelHeaderMode')?$('excelHeaderMode').value:'YES';
  var note='Format: '+fmt+' — automatic column detection';
  if(hm==='NO')note+=' — positional/raw data';
  if($('excelStatus')&&excelWorkbook)$('excelStatus').setAttribute('data-mapping',note);
}
function applyExcelFormat(){setupExcelMapping();}
function excelCurrentRows(){return excelRows||[];}
function excelCell(row,idx){return idx>=0?row[idx]:'';}
/* V481: robust numeric parsing for Excel cells exported with Persian/Arabic digits,
   localized decimal separators, or non-breaking spaces. */
function excelNum(v){
 if(typeof v==='number')return isFinite(v)?v:NaN;
 var s=String(v==null?'':v).trim();if(!s)return NaN;
 var fa='۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹',ar='٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩';
 s=s.replace(/[۰-۹]/g,function(c){return String(fa.indexOf(c));}).replace(/[٠-٩]/g,function(c){return String(ar.indexOf(c));});
 s=s.replace(/[٬   ]/g,'');
 /* Treat comma as decimal when it is the only separator; otherwise as thousands separator. */
 if(s.indexOf(',')>=0&&s.indexOf('.')<0){var parts=s.split(',');s=parts.length===2&&parts[1].length!==3?parts[0]+'.'+parts[1]:parts.join('');}
 else s=s.replace(/,/g,'');
 s=s.replace(/[٫]/g,'.').replace(/[−–]/g,'-');
 var n=Number(s);return isFinite(n)?n:NaN;
}
var excelWorkMode='TABLE';
function excelResolveMode(){
 var requested=$('excelViewMode')?$('excelViewMode').value:'AUTO';
 if(requested==='TABLE')return 'TABLE';
 if(requested==='SURVEY')return 'SURVEY';
 /* AUTO is intentionally conservative: only explicit Easting/X AND Northing/Y
    headers classify a workbook as surveying. Ordinary numeric ledgers stay tables. */
 var hasE=false,hasN=false;
 for(var i=0;i<excelHeaders.length;i++){var role=excelHeaderRole(excelHeaders[i]||'');if(role==='E')hasE=true;if(role==='N')hasN=true;}
 return hasE&&hasN?'SURVEY':'TABLE';
}
function excelSetModeUI(){
 var table=excelWorkMode==='TABLE',actions=$('excelTableActions');
 if(actions)actions.style.display=table?'block':'none';
 var note=$('m6eOutputStatus');if(note)note.textContent=table?'حالت اکسل معمولی فعال است؛ جدول اصلی بدون تبدیل PENZD نمایش داده می‌شود.':'حالت نقشه‌برداری فعال است؛ خروجی‌ها از داده‌های تشخیص‌داده‌شده ساخته می‌شوند.';
 var ids=['excelOutStandard','excelOutPDF','excelOutPENZD','excelOutPNEZD','excelOutNEZ','excelOutENZ','excelOutLEICA','excelOutKML','excelOutKMZ','excelOutDXF','excelOutLandXML','excelOutGeoJSON','excelOutCode','excelOutJSON','excelOutTXT'];
 for(var j=0;j<ids.length;j++){var b=$(ids[j]);if(b)b.disabled=table;}
 var x=$('excelOutXLSX'),xl=$('excelOutXLS'),csv=$('excelOutCSV');
 if(x)x.disabled=table?false:!(typeof points!=='undefined'&&points.length);
 if(xl)xl.disabled=table?true:!(typeof points!=='undefined'&&points.length);
 if(csv)csv.disabled=table?true:!(typeof points!=='undefined'&&points.length);
}
function applyExcelViewMode(){
 if(!excelWorkbook)return false;
 excelWorkMode=excelResolveMode();
 if(excelWorkMode==='SURVEY'){
   previewExcelRows();var parsed=excelRowsToPoints();
   if(parsed.points&&parsed.points.length){importExcelPoints(true);setExcelOutputsEnabled(true);}
   if($('excelStatus'))$('excelStatus').textContent='حالت نقشه‌برداری فعال شد؛ '+(parsed.points?parsed.points.length:0)+' نقطه شناسایی شد.';
 }else{
   if($('excelPreview'))excelRenderTablePreview();
   setExcelOutputsEnabled(false);excelSetModeUI();
   if($('excelStatus'))$('excelStatus').textContent='حالت اکسل معمولی فعال شد؛ اطلاعات اصلی بدون تبدیل به نقاط نمایش داده می‌شود.';
 }
 m6eSync();return false;
}
function excelRenderTablePreview(){
 var box=$('excelPreview');if(!box)return;
 var rows=excelRows||[],heads=excelHeaders||[],maxCols=heads.length;
 for(var i=0;i<rows.length;i++)maxCols=Math.max(maxCols,(rows[i]||[]).length);
 var html='<div class="m6e-note"><b>نمایش اکسل معمولی</b><br>ساختار و ترتیب ستون‌ها حفظ شده است. هیچ داده‌ای به PENZD تبدیل نمی‌شود.<br><small>'+rows.length+' ردیف داده | '+maxCols+' ستون | '+(excelWorkbook&&excelWorkbook.SheetNames?excelWorkbook.SheetNames.length:0)+' Sheet</small></div><div class="m6e-table-scroll"><table><thead><tr>';
 for(var c=0;c<maxCols;c++)html+='<th>'+escapeHTML(String(heads[c]||('ستون '+(c+1))))+'</th>';
 html+='<th class="m6e-row-actions-head">عملیات</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
 var limit=Math.min(rows.length,250);
 for(var r=0;r<limit;r++){
  html+='<tr>';
  for(var k=0;k<maxCols;k++){var val=rows[r]&&rows[r][k]!=null?rows[r][k]:'';html+='<td>'+escapeHTML(String(val))+'</td>';}
  html+='<td class="m6e-row-actions-cell"><button type="button" class="m6e-row-action" onclick="return m6eEditRow('+r+');">✎ ویرایش</button><button type="button" class="m6e-row-action delete" onclick="return m6eDeleteRow('+r+');">حذف</button></td></tr>';
 }
 html+='</tbody></table></div><div class="subtitle m6e-preview-note">'+rows.length+' ردیف'+(rows.length>limit?' — نمایش '+limit+' ردیف اول':'')+'</div>';
 box.innerHTML=html;
 if($('m6eReviewStatus'))$('m6eReviewStatus').textContent='جدول اصلی آماده مشاهده است؛ هر ردیف از همین‌جا قابل ویرایش یا حذف است.';
 excelSetModeUI();
}

}
